title:PvE Podcast 173 - The Best Fusion Rifles
description:Monument of Triumph new and reprised fusion and linear fusion rifles, based on trait perk discussions between @CourtProjects and @ImpetusAlways in the 30 June 2026 @PodvsEnemies podcast (https://open.spotify.com/episode/4xT8SsWzAeAoUXY4Dq1A80)

// Mistral Lift - PvE Boss good 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery, Ionized Battery), (Envious Assassin, Overflow, Reconstruction), (Bait and Switch)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Mistral Lift is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are low by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/270610849/mistral-lift and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/270610849 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is slightly right and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with a Backup Mag mod or Ionized Battery gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you insist, in the first trait perk column, Overflow refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2 of base (×2.2 when enhanced), rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick, or Reconstruction refills 25% of the battery every 6s (5.5s enhanced) up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. For damage, in the second column Reservoir Burst provides a ×1.3 (×1.33 enhanced) damage boost when the battery is at or above maximum base capacity and triggers an AoE explosion on a kill, as well as passively granting +30 to Battery Capacity - the equivalent of a Backup Mag mod. The Veist Stinger is the best choice of origin trait for a boss damage roll, providing a 10% chance per hit to refill 25% of the battery, or Dawning Surprise has a hidden counter - 6 red bar kills (5 enhanced) or 2 orange/yellow bars will fill this to generate a pickup which lasts 10s that grants 10% energy to melee, grenade and super, +22 HP and start regeneration, so is less useful here. If you're interested, a mistral is a strong, cold, north-westerly wind that blows through France into the Mediterranean. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Mistral Lift - PvE Boss good 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery, Ionized Battery), (Overflow, Reconstruction), (Reservoir Burst, Precision Instrument)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Mistral Lift is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are low by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/270610849/mistral-lift and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/270610849 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is slightly right and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with a Backup Mag mod or Ionized Battery gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you insist, in the first trait perk column, Overflow refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2 of base (×2.2 when enhanced), rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick, or Reconstruction refills 25% of the battery every 6s (5.5s enhanced) up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. For damage, in the second column Reservoir Burst provides a ×1.3 (×1.33 enhanced) damage boost when the battery is at or above maximum base capacity and triggers an AoE explosion on a kill, as well as passively granting +30 to Battery Capacity - the equivalent of a Backup Mag mod. Alternatively, Precision Instrument grants up to ×6 stacks of escalating damage on repeated crit hits up to a max ×1.25 (×1.3 enhanced) for 1.25s or until you miss a crit. The Veist Stinger is the best choice of origin trait for a boss damage roll, providing a 10% chance per hit to refill 25% of the battery, or Dawning Surprise has a hidden counter - 6 red bar kills (5 enhanced) or 2 orange/yellow bars will fill this to generate a pickup which lasts 10s that grants 10% energy to melee, grenade and super, +22 HP and start regeneration, so is less useful here. If you're interested, a mistral is a strong, cold, north-westerly wind that blows through France into the Mediterranean. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Mistral Lift - PvE Utility god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery, Ionized Battery), (Withering Gaze), (Rewind Rounds)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice niche PvE utility roll for maintaining Weaken in a fireteam for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Mistral Lift is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are low by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/270610849/mistral-lift and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/270610849 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is slightly right and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with a Backup Mag mod or Ionized Battery gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you need to maintain Void Weaken from, say a Deadfall Shadowshot, then Withering Gaze in the first trait perk slot can apply the Void Weaken debuff to targets after ADS-ing for 1.4s (1.3s enhanced) for ×1.15 damage for a refreshable 6s for your fireteam, but if the stronger ×1.3 weaken has been applied from Tractor Cannon or Shadowshot, a proc will reapply at the higher value, while in the second column, Rewind Rounds refills 60% of hits landed (70% when enhanced) from reserves when the battery empties. You can contribute to damage with special weapon in between shots. The Veist Stinger is the best choice of origin trait for a boss damage roll, providing a 10% chance per hit to refill 25% of the battery, or Dawning Surprise has a hidden counter - 6 red bar kills (5 enhanced) or 2 orange/yellow bars will fill this to generate a pickup which lasts 10s that grants 10% energy to melee, grenade and super, +22 HP and start regeneration, so is less useful here. If you're interested, a mistral is a strong, cold, north-westerly wind that blows through France into the Mediterranean. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Mistral Lift - PvE Boss good 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery, Ionized Battery), (Envious Assassin, Overflow, Reconstruction), (Bait and Switch)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Mistral Lift is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are low by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/270610849/mistral-lift and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/270610849 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is slightly right and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with a Backup Mag mod or Ionized Battery gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you insist, in the first trait perk column, Overflow refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2 of base (×2.2 when enhanced), rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick, or Reconstruction refills 25% of the battery every 6s (5.5s enhanced) up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. For damage, in the second column Reservoir Burst provides a ×1.3 (×1.33 enhanced) damage boost when the battery is at or above maximum base capacity and triggers an AoE explosion on a kill, as well as passively granting +30 to Battery Capacity - the equivalent of a Backup Mag mod. The Veist Stinger is the best choice of origin trait for a boss damage roll, providing a 10% chance per hit to refill 25% of the battery, or Dawning Surprise has a hidden counter - 6 red bar kills (5 enhanced) or 2 orange/yellow bars will fill this to generate a pickup which lasts 10s that grants 10% energy to melee, grenade and super, +22 HP and start regeneration, so is less useful here. If you're interested, a mistral is a strong, cold, north-westerly wind that blows through France into the Mediterranean. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

The recoil direction is slightly right and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with a Backup Mag mod or Ionized Battery gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you need to maintain Void Weaken from, say a Deadfall Shadowshot, then Withering Gaze in the first trait perk slot can apply the Void Weaken debuff to targets after ADS-ing for 1.4s (1.3s enhanced) for ×1.15 damage for a refreshable 6s for your fireteam, but if the stronger ×1.3 weaken has been applied from Tractor Cannon or Shadowshot, a proc will reapply at the higher value, while in the second column, Rewind Rounds refills 60% of hits landed (70% when enhanced) from reserves when the battery empties. You can contribute to damage with special weapon in between shots. The Veist Stinger is the best choice of origin trait for a boss damage roll, providing a 10% chance per hit to refill 25% of the battery, or Dawning Surprise has a hidden counter - 6 red bar kills (5 enhanced) or 2 orange/yellow bars will fill this to generate a pickup which lasts 10s that grants 10% energy to melee, grenade and super, +22 HP and start regeneration, so is less useful here. If you're interested, a mistral is a strong, cold, north-westerly wind that blows through France into the Mediterranean. // Loaded Question - PvE Boss god 1
// (Extended Barrel, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling, Corkscrew Rifling, Fluted Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery, Ionized Battery), (|Overflow, Envious Assassin, Demolitionist, Auto-Loading Holster), (Controlled Burst)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Loaded Question is a high impact fusion rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "strongest standout for body and ranged QoL DPS and a big step up over other fusions". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, and a ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when ADS and moving slower that 2.8m/s - crouch walking speed - as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are good by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/852069910/loaded-question and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/852069910 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The Recoil Direction is a bit right and fairly tight, but with only 5 bolts to control, you could look to boost Range with the barrel perk, which helps to a degree with recoil anyway, or to boost your preferred stat. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced or Ionized Battery with an Backup Mag mod gets you to 7 or the maximum of 8 shots respectively. High-impacts provide the best boss total damage and DPS among fusions. You can overflow the battery with Overflow which refills the magazine and boosts its capacity to ×2 of base (×2.2 when enhanced), rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick, or Envious Assassin in the first trait perk column, which overflows the magazine to up to ×3 capacity at 10% of mag size per kill with primary weapons and 20% with heavy (12.5%/22.5% enhanced) before readying. Alternatively, Demolitionist provides an instant magazine refill on grenade use and generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills or Auto-Loading Holster refills the magazine 3.5s (3.3s enhanced) after stowing. In the second column, for damage, Controlled Burst reduces charge time to ×0.9 and boosts damage by ×1.2 for 2s (2.5s when enhanced) after a kill or landing all the bolts in a burst. The Problem Solver origin trait is very good for PvE when facing yellow bars - 12 hits (4 bursts) will apply Exhaust to an enemy, reducing damage output to ×0.9 for a refreshable 5s and granting +50 to Reload Speed and Handling for 10s (12s when enhanced) - both effects are refreshable. Otherwise, Vanguard Determination provides up to ×3 stacks that reduces incoming damage to max ×0.94 for a refreshable 10s (12.5s when enhanced), on kills within 20s of using a class ability. If you're interested, "Loaded Question" has a deeper, tragic-comic meaning tied directly to Cayde-6 and his relationship with the gunsmith, described in the lore and the flavour text. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time or Backup Mag mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Cataclysmic - PvE Boss god 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Arrowhead Brake, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Fourth Time's the Charm), (Elemental Honing, Aggregate Charge)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvP-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Prismatic builds. Cataclysmic is a precision linear fusion rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic for "total damage standout, notable for solo due to less reliance on reload/Div vs. A499 and Particle access". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are good by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/999767358/cataclysmic and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/999767358will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. With precision frames, you need to worry less about recoil control so look to improve Handling or maybe Stability. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Ionized Battery in the battery perk slot gets you the maximum of 7 shots. For sustained boss damage, in the first trait perk column, Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 2s (3s when enhanced) of each other - that's 10 shots from a battery of 6, or 11 from a battery of 7, with 4 of them free. In the second column, for damage on a Prismatic build, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to an max ×1.35 for 20s (23s when enhanced) for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5, or or Aggregate Charge provides a stack for each different elemental debuff up to ×3 stacks to boost damage by a max ×1.3 (×1.33 when enhanced), with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active. The Souldrinker origin trait restores up to +50HP on reload, depending on the proportion of the magazine landed as hits. If you're interested, community consensus is that the Vow of the Disciple raid's weapon shapes such as "Fatal", referenced in the flavour text, represent stages in the Witness's and Rhulk's philosophy regarding the universe. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvP-God M+KB Controller

//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvP-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Cataclysmic is a precision linear fusion rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic for "total damage standout, notable for solo due to less reliance on reload/Div vs. A499 and Particle access". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are good by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/999767358/cataclysmic and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/999767358will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. With precision frames, you need to worry less about recoil control so look to improve Handling or maybe Stability. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Ionized Battery in the battery perk slot gets you the maximum of 7 shots. For sustained boss damage, in the first trait perk column, Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 2s (3s when enhanced) of each other - that's 10 shots from a battery of 6, or 11 from a battery of 7, with 4 of them free. In the second column for damage, Bait and Switch boosts damage by ×1.3 for 10s (11s when enhanced) for doing damage with your other two weapons, or Focused Fury boosts damage by ×1.25 for a refreshable 11s (12s enhanced) or until reload or stow, after hitting 35% of base capacity as precision hits - that's 3. The Souldrinker origin trait restores up to +50HP on reload, depending on the proportion of the magazine landed as hits. If you're interested, community consensus is that the Vow of the Disciple raid's weapon shapes such as "Fatal", referenced in the flavour text, represent stages in the Witness's and Rhulk's philosophy regarding the universe. //notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvP-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Cataclysmic is a precision linear fusion rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic for "total damage standout, notable for solo due to less reliance on reload/Div vs. A499 and Particle access". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are good by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/2886339027/cataclysmic and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2886339027 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. With precision frames, you need to worry less about recoil control so look to improve Handling or maybe Stability. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Ionized Battery in the battery perk slot gets you the maximum of 7 shots. For sustained boss damage, in the first trait perk column, Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 2s (3s when enhanced) of each other - that's 10 shots from a battery of 6, or 11 from a battery of 7, with 4 of them free. In the second column for damage, Bait and Switch boosts damage by ×1.3 for 10s (11s when enhanced) for doing damage with your other two weapons, or Focused Fury boosts damage by ×1.25 for a refreshable 11s (12s enhanced) or until reload or stow, after hitting 35% of base capacity as precision hits - that's 3. The Souldrinker origin trait restores up to +50HP on reload, depending on the proportion of the magazine landed as hits. If you're interested, community consensus is that the Vow of the Disciple raid's weapon shapes such as "Fatal", referenced in the flavour text, represent stages in the Witness's and Rhulk's philosophy regarding the universe. //notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Scintillation is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and having no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/1207321710/scintillation and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1207321710 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is a little right vertical and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery gets you to 6 shots, and then the maximum 7 with a Bandolier or Backup Mag mod. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you insist, in the first trait perk column, Rewind Rounds refills 60% of hits landed (70% when enhanced) from reserves when the magazine empties or Envious Assassin overflows the magazine to up to ×3 capacity at 10% of mag size per kill with primary weapons and 20% with specials (12.5%/22.5% enhanced) before readying, preloading pretty much all of your ammo reserves for killing adds. In the second column for damage, Bait and Switch boosts damage by ×1.3 for 10s (11s enhanced) for doing damage with your other two weapons or Firing Line boosts crit damage by ×1.2 when you're within 15m of two or more allies. There's two strong origin traits, Veist Stinger, which provides a 10% chance per hit to refill 25% of the magazine and Problem Solver, which will apply Exhaust to an enemy after 12 hits (4 bursts), reducing damage output to ×0.9 for a refreshable 5s and granting +50 to Reload Speed and Handling for 10s (12s when enhanced) - both effects are refreshable. The Vanguard Determination origin trait is the weakest for a boss DPS roll, providing up to ×3 stacks that reduces incoming damage to max ×0.94 for a refreshable 10s (12.5s when enhanced), on kills within 20s of using a class ability. If you're interested, "scintillation" refers to a flash of light produced when high-energy particles or radiation strike a luminescent material, or the twinkling of starlight caused by atmospheric turbulence. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time mod or your preferred choice boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Iterative Loop - PvE Boss good 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Fluted Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Smallbore, Corkscrew Rifling, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Overflow, Auto-Loading Holster), (Controlled Burst)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty on Arc builds. Iterative Loop is a rapid-fire fusion rifle, which are rated F-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being a "strict downgrade from high-impact and precision frames for boss damage". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the battery is empty as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/1289796511/iterative-loop and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1289796511 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. With 9 bolts to control, look for a barrel perk that helps with recoil control together with any other stat that you want to bump. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery gets you to the maximum of 7 shots. While losing out to precision and high-impact frames for total boss damage, if you wanted one for ease of use, then on Arc builds, Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the magazine every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff is active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity. In the second column, for damage, Controlled Burst reduces charge time to ×0.9 and boosts damage by ×1.2 for 2s (2.5s when enhanced) after a kill or landing all the bolts in a burst. The Nanotech Tracer Missile origin trait generates a tracking elemental micro missile for extra damage for landing 3 bursts on a target within 6s of each other - with a 2s cooldown. If you're interested, in computer science and mathematics, an iterative loop is a process that repeats a stepwise routine, gradually refining, adjusting, or updating an object or code toward a desired result until perfection is approached. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/1491665733/briars-contempt and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1491665733 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is slightly left and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with a Backup Mag mod or Ionized Battery gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you insist, in the first trait perk column, Envious Assassin overflows the battery to up to ×3 capacity at 10% of base size per kill with primary weapons and 20% with specials (12.5%/22.5% when enhanced), so you can preload pretty much all your reserves before readying, Rewind Rounds refills 60% of hits landed (70% enhanced) from reserves when the battery empties, or Reconstruction refills 25% of the battery every 6s (5.5s enhanced) up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. For damage with Prismatic builds, in the second column Aggregate Charge provides a stack for each different elemental debuff up to ×3 stacks to boost damage to a max ×1.3 (×1.33 enhanced), with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active. The Harmonic Resonance origin trait boosts Handling and Reload Speed, and damage by up to a max ×1.1 against Dread and Lucent Hive Lightbearers for equipping other Root of Nightmares weapons. If you're interested, Briar was a former solar Titan, who was driven to madness and broken by Nezarec, before joining the Acolytes of Nezarec. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Briar's Contempt - PvE Boss good 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery, Ionized Battery), (Envious Assassin, Rewind Rounds, Reconstruction), (Chaos Reshaped, Focused Fury, Surrounded)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Briar's Contempt is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/1491665733/briars-contempt and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1491665733 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is slightly left and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with a Backup Mag mod or Ionized Battery gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you insist, in the first trait perk column, Envious Assassin overflows the battery to up to ×3 capacity at 10% of base size per kill with primary weapons and 20% with specials (12.5%/22.5% when enhanced), so you can preload pretty much all your reserves before readying, Rewind Rounds refills 60% of hits landed (70% enhanced) from reserves when the battery empties, or Reconstruction refills 25% of the battery every 6s (5.5s enhanced) up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. For damage, in the second column Chaos Reshaped stacks up to ×2 on dealing or receiving damage within a 5s window for at least 12s (11.5s enhanced) - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration or Focused Fury boosts damage by ×1.25 for a refreshable 11s (12s enhanced) or until reload or stow, after hitting 35% of base capacity as precision hits - that's 3. More niche, for damage phases where there are adds around you, Surrounded boosts damage by up to to ×1.4 (×1.47 enhanced) while within 15m of 3+ enemies. The Harmonic Resonance origin trait boosts Handling and Reload Speed, and damage by up to a max ×1.1 against Dread and Lucent Hive Lightbearers for equipping other Root of Nightmares weapons. If you're interested, Briar was a former solar Titan, who was driven to madness and broken by Nezarec, before joining the Acolytes of Nezarec. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Briar's Contempt (Adept) - PvE Boss good 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery, Ionized Battery), (Envious Assassin, Rewind Rounds, Reconstruction), (Aggregate Charge)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly for Prismatic builds. Briar's Contempt is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/2890082420/briars-contempt and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2890082420 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is slightly left and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with a Backup Mag mod or Ionized Battery gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you insist, in the first trait perk column, Envious Assassin overflows the battery to up to ×3 capacity at 10% of base size per kill with primary weapons and 20% with specials (12.5%/22.5% when enhanced), so you can preload pretty much all your reserves before readying, Rewind Rounds refills 60% of hits landed (70% enhanced) from reserves when the battery empties, or Reconstruction refills 25% of the battery every 6s (5.5s enhanced) up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. For damage with Prismatic builds, in the second column Aggregate Charge provides a stack for each different elemental debuff up to ×3 stacks to boost damage to a max ×1.3 (×1.33 enhanced), with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active. The Harmonic Resonance origin trait boosts Handling and Reload Speed, and damage by up to a max ×1.1 against Dread and Lucent Hive Lightbearers for equipping other Root of Nightmares weapons. If you're interested, Briar was a former solar Titan, who was driven to madness and broken by Nezarec, before joining the Acolytes of Nezarec. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Briar's Contempt (Adept) - PvE Boss good 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery, Ionized Battery), (Envious Assassin, Rewind Rounds, Reconstruction), (Chaos Reshaped, Focused Fury, Surrounded)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Briar's Contempt is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/2890082420/briars-contempt and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2890082420 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is slightly left and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with a Backup Mag mod or Ionized Battery gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you insist, in the first trait perk column, Envious Assassin overflows the battery to up to ×3 capacity at 10% of base size per kill with primary weapons and 20% with specials (12.5%/22.5% when enhanced), so you can preload pretty much all your reserves before readying, Rewind Rounds refills 60% of hits landed (70% enhanced) from reserves when the battery empties, or Reconstruction refills 25% of the battery every 6s (5.5s enhanced) up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. For damage, in the second column Chaos Reshaped stacks up to ×2 on dealing or receiving damage within a 5s window for at least 12s (11.5s enhanced) - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration or Focused Fury boosts damage by ×1.25 for a refreshable 11s (12s enhanced) or until reload or stow, after hitting 35% of base capacity as precision hits - that's 3. More niche, for damage phases where there are adds around you, Surrounded boosts damage by up to to ×1.4 (×1.47 enhanced) while within 15m of 3+ enemies. The Harmonic Resonance origin trait boosts Handling and Reload Speed, and damage by up to a max ×1.1 against Dread and Lucent Hive Lightbearers for equipping other Root of Nightmares weapons. If you're interested, Briar was a former solar Titan, who was driven to madness and broken by Nezarec, before joining the Acolytes of Nezarec. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Willful Hamartia - PvE Boss good 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Envious Assassin), (Bait and Switch, Reservoir Burst, Precision Instrument, Jolting Feedback)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Willful Hamartia is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are good by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/1952295804/willful-hamartia and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1952295804 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is vertical and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with Backup Mag in the battery perk slot gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you insist, in the first trait perk column, Envious Assassin overflows the battery to up to ×3 capacity at 10% of mag size per kill with primary weapons and 20% with specials (12.5%/22.5% when enhanced) before readying, during add phases. For damage, in the second column Bait and Switch boosts damage by ×1.3 for 10s (11s when enhanced) for doing damage with your other two weapons, or Reservoir Burst provides a ×1.3 (×1.33 when enhanced) damage boost when the battery is at or above maximum base capacity and triggers an AoE explosion on a kill, as well as passively granting +30 to Battery Capacity - the equivalent of a Backup Mag mod. Alternatively, Precision Instrument grants up to ×6 stacks of escalating damage on repeated crit hits up to a max ×1.25 (×1.3 enhanced) for 1.25s or until you miss a crit, or Jolting Feedback applies Arc Jolt after 4 hits (3 enhanced or when amplified) to tougher enemies who can survive the weapon damage, triggering chain lightning on further hits for both target and AoE damage and useful for others on your fireteam using Aggregate Charge weapons. For a burst damage roll, the Roar of Battle origin trait is probably most useful, granting +10 Stability and Handling for 6s after dealing or taking damage every 0.5s or so for 2s. Against Elites, Champions and Minibosses, Wild Card provides the chance of Telesto-like "Experimental Submunitions" dropping on a kill - the number depends on the enemy tier - that last up to 10.5s and explode on contact or proximity to an enemy. The least useful for this roll is Fleet Footed, which stacks up to ×1 on hits and ×2 on kills to boost movement speed and Airborne Effectiveness for a refreshable 5s (6s enhanced). If you're interested, hamartia is an ancient Greek term meaning a fatal flaw, error in judgment, or "missing the mark" (originally used in archery) that brings about a hero's downfall in classical tragedy, so "Willful Hamartia" essentially translates to a deliberate fatal mistake or chosen tragic flaw. Instead of a tragic hero accidentally stumbling into their doom, it implies choosing the path of error with open eyes. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time or Backup Mag mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Willful Hamartia - PvE Boss good 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Rapid Hit, High-Impact Reserves), (Bait and Switch, Precision Instrument, Jolting Feedback)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for burst damage against a a Bane, Elite, Champion or Miniboss, or shorter boss DPS windows in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Willful Hamartia is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are good by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/1952295804/willful-hamartia and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1952295804 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is vertical and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with Backup Mag in the battery perk slot gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for damage but, if you insist, in the first trait perk column, Rapid Hit stacks up to ×5 on crit hits for a max +25 Stability and +60 Reload Speed with a ×0.92 animation multiplier for a refreshable 2s or, for double damage perks, High Impact Reserves boosts damage from ×1.121 to ×1.256 as the battery drops below 50% (55% when enhanced). For damage, in the second column Precision Instrument grants escalating damage on repeated crit hits up to a max ×1.25 at 6 stacks (×1.3 enhanced) for 1.25s or until you miss a crit, or Jolting Feedback applies Arc Jolt after 4 hits (3 enhanced or when amplified) to tougher enemies who can survive the weapon damage, triggering chain lightning on further hits for both target and AoE damage and useful for others on your fireteam using Aggregate Charge weapons. For a boss damage roll, the Roar of Battle origin trait is probably most useful, granting +10 Stability and Handling for 6s after dealing or taking damage every 0.5s or so for 2s. Against Elites, Champions and Minibosses, Wild Card provides the chance of Telesto-like "Experimental Submunitions" dropping on a kill - the number depends on the enemy tier - that last up to 10.5s and explode on contact or proximity to an enemy. The least useful for this roll is Fleet Footed, which stacks up to ×1 on hits and ×2 on kills to boost movement speed and Airborne Effectiveness for a refreshable 5s (6s enhanced). If you're interested, hamartia is an ancient Greek term meaning a fatal flaw, error in judgment, or "missing the mark" (originally used in archery) that brings about a hero's downfall in classical tragedy, so "Willful Hamartia" essentially translates to a deliberate fatal mistake or chosen tragic flaw. Instead of a tragic hero accidentally stumbling into their doom, it implies choosing the path of error with open eyes. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time or Backup Mag mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Null Composure - PvE Boss good 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Corkscrew Rifling, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Overflow, Reconstruction, Envious Assassin), (Elemental Honing)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Prismatic builds. Null Composure is a rapid-fire fusion rifle, which are rated F-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being a "strict downgrade from high-impact and precision frames for boss damage". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the battery is empty as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/3613444087/null-composure and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3613444087 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. With 9 bolts to control, look for a barrel perk that helps with recoil control together with any other stat that you want to bump. Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage or Enhanced Battery, with a Backup Mag mod, or Ionized Battery in the battery perk slot gets you to the maximum of 8 shots. While losing out to precision and high-impact frames for total boss damage, if you wanted one for ease of use, in the first trait perk slot, you can overflow the battery with either Overflow which refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2 of base (×2.2 when enhanced), rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick, or Reconstruction to refill 25% of the battery every 6s (5.5s when enhanced) up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. Otherwise, Envious Arsenal refills the battery from reserves on dealing damage with both of your other weapons as part of a damage weapon rotation. In the second column for damage, for Prismatic synergy, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to ×1.35 for 20s (23s when enhanced) for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. If you're lucky you'll drop one with the Accelerated Assault origin trait, which passively reloads 10% of the battery and boosts damage by ×1.05 for getting 3 hits within a 1.6s window. But, if not, Problem Solver is very good for PvE when facing yellow bars - 12 bolt hits will apply Exhaust to an enemy, reducing damage output to ×0.9 for a refreshable 5s and granting +50 to Reload Speed and Handling for 10s (12s when enhanced) - both effects are refreshable. Least useful is Vanguard Determination which provides up to ×3 stacks that reduces incoming damage to max ×0.94 for a refreshable 10s (12.5s when enhanced), on kills within 20s of using a class ability. If you're interested, the name is a Season of the Splicer hidden reference to the 1995 cult classic hacking movie "Hackers", serving as an alternate way to say the hacker handle "Zero Cool". Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time or Backup Mag mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Null Composure - PvE Boss good 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Corkscrew Rifling, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Overflow, Reconstruction, Envious Assassin), (Elemental Honing)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Prismatic builds. Null Composure is a rapid-fire fusion rifle, which are rated F-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being a "strict downgrade from high-impact and precision frames for boss damage". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the battery is empty as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/3613444087/null-composure and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3613444087 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. With 9 bolts to control, look for a barrel perk that helps with recoil control together with any other stat that you want to bump. Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage or Enhanced Battery, with a Backup Mag mod, or Ionized Battery in the battery perk slot gets you to the maximum of 8 shots. While losing out to precision and high-impact frames for total boss damage, if you wanted one for ease of use, in the first trait perk slot, you can overflow the battery with either Overflow which refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2 of base (×2.2 when enhanced), rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick, or Reconstruction to refill 25% of the battery every 6s (5.5s when enhanced) up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. Otherwise, Envious Arsenal refills the battery from reserves on dealing damage with both of your other weapons as part of a damage weapon rotation. In the second column for damage, for Prismatic synergy, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to ×1.35 for 20s (23s when enhanced) for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. If you're lucky you'll drop one with the Accelerated Assault origin trait, which passively reloads 10% of the battery and boosts damage by ×1.05 for getting 3 hits within a 1.6s window. But, if not, Problem Solver is very good for PvE when facing yellow bars - 12 bolt hits will apply Exhaust to an enemy, reducing damage output to ×0.9 for a refreshable 5s and granting +50 to Reload Speed and Handling for 10s (12s when enhanced) - both effects are refreshable. Least useful is Vanguard Determination which provides up to ×3 stacks that reduces incoming damage to max ×0.94 for a refreshable 10s (12.5s when enhanced), on kills within 20s of using a class ability. If you're interested, the name is a Season of the Splicer hidden reference to the 1995 cult classic hacking movie "Hackers", serving as an alternate way to say the hacker handle "Zero Cool". Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time or Backup Mag mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// The Beacon - PvE Boss good 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Corkscrew Rifling, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Envious Arsenal, Overflow, Rewind Rounds, Demolitionist), (Bait and Switch, Controlled Burst)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. The Beacon is a rapid-fire fusion rifle, which are rated F-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being a "strict downgrade from high-impact and precision frames for boss damage". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the battery is empty as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are good by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/76739872/the-beacon and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/76739872 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. With 9 bolts to control, look for a barrel perk that helps with recoil control together with any other stat that you want to bump. Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage or Enhanced Battery in the battery perk slot gets you to the maximum of 8 shots. While losing out to precision and high-impact frames for total boss damage, if you wanted one for ease of use, in the first trait perk slot, Envious Arsenal refills the battery from reserves on dealing damage with both of your other weapons, Overflow refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2 of base (×2.2 when enhanced), rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick, Rewind Rounds refills 60% of hits landed (70% enhanced) from reserves when the battery empties or Demolitionist provides an instant battery refill on grenade use and generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills. In the second column, for damage, Bait and Switch boosts damage by ×1.3 for 10s (11s enhanced) for doing damage with your other two weapons as part of a weapon damage rotation, or Controlled Burst reduces charge time to ×0.9 and boosts damage by ×1.2 for 2s (2.5s enhanced) after a kill or landing all the bolts in a burst. The origin trait is particularly useful for a boss roll - Classy Contender provides 5% (6% enhanced) Class ability energy on kills. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Fire and Forget - PvE Boss good 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Field Prep), (Focused Fury)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Fire and Forget is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are okay by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/2272041093/fire-and-forget and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2272041093 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is vertical and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with Backup Mag gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, there aren't any great Stasis LFRs within an already weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you insist, in the first trait perk column, Field Prep grants +50 Reload Speed with a ×0.8 animation scalar to both reload and ready/stow when crouched, and bumps Ammo Generation by +30 (+55 Reload Speed and +40 Ammo Generation when enhanced). For damage, in the second column Focused Fury boosts damage by ×1.25 for a refreshable 11s (12s enhanced) or until reload or stow, after hitting 35% of the mag as precision hits - that's 8 crits from 3 bursts. The Ambush origin trait improves both Range and Handling by +20 (+22 enhanced) and damage by ×1.088 for 2s after first dealing or receiving damage, or Veist Stinger provides a 10% chance per hit to refill 25% of the magazine. If you're interested, Fire and Forget has a double meaning tied to self-guiding munitions, and the nature of Exo mind wipes in dealing with past emotional trauma. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time or Backup Mag mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Fire and Forget - PvE Utility good 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Field Prep), (Chill Clip)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-Champion PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE utility roll to deal with all Champion types in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly for Stasis builds. Fire and Forget is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are okay by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/2272041093/fire-and-forget and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2272041093 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is vertical and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with Backup Mag gets you the maximum of 7 shots. For your anti-Champion roll, there's not much that helps in the first trait perk column - Field Prep grants +50 Reload Speed with a ×0.8 animation scalar to both reload and ready/stow when crouched, and bumps Ammo Generation by +30 (+55 Reload Speed and +40 Ammo Generation when enhanced). In the second column for the "stun every Champion" roll, Chill Clip applies +60 stacks of slow (freezing targets after 2 hits) within a 4m AoE for 4s on a hit - 8s with Whisper of Durance - with ammo in the top half of the base battery size. You'll pierce Barriers with the frame intrinsic, disrupt Overloads with Slow, and shattering after freezing an Unstoppable will stun it, so you can handle all champion types with one weapon. The Ambush origin trait improves both Range and Handling by +20 (+22 enhanced) and damage by ×1.088 for 2s after first dealing or receiving damage, or Veist Stinger provides a 10% chance per hit to refill 25% of the magazine. If you're interested, Fire and Forget has a double meaning tied to self-guiding munitions, and the nature of Exo mind wipes in dealing with past emotional trauma. Recommended MW: Charge Time, a Charge Time or Backup Mag mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-Champion PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Riptide - PvE Utility god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Corkscrew Rifling, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Overflow), (Chill Clip)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-Champion PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE utility roll to deal with Overload and Unstoppable Champions in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly for Stasis builds. Riptide is a rapid-fire fusion rifle, which are rated F-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being a "strict downgrade from high-impact and precision frames for boss damage". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the battery is empty as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are good by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/2297554989/riptide and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2297554989 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. With 9 bolts to control, look for a barrel perk that helps with recoil control together with any other stat that you want to bump. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery gets you to the maximum of 8 shots. To replace your older utility Riptide - Overflow in the first trait perk column overflows the battery by ×2 (×2.2 when enhanced), rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick. That pairs well in the second column with Chill Clip which applies +40 stacks of slow (freezing targets after 3 hits) within a 4m AoE for 4s on a hit - 8s with Whisper of Durance - with ammo in the top half of the base battery size. With an overflowed battery, that means 10 Chill Clip shots. Alternatively, Crystalline Corpsebloom procs on a kill to provide a 4s buff - kills with the buff active generate a Stasis seeker which travels 3m before spawning a medium Stasis crystal (large when enhanced) - these are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. You'll already disrupt Overloads with the frame intrinsic, and shattering after freezing an Unstoppable will stun it, so you can handle two champion types with one weapon. If you're lucky you'll drop one with the Accelerated Assault origin trait, which passively reloads 10% of the battery and boosts damage by ×1.05 for getting 3 hits within a 1.6s window. Otherwise, there's a choice of Wild Card for the chance of Telesto-like "Experimental Submunitions" dropping on a kill - the number depends on the enemy tier - that last up to 10.5s and explode on contact or proximity to an enemy or Roar of Battle which grants +10 Stability and Handling for 6s after dealing or taking damage every 0.5s or so for 2s. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-Champion PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Nox Perennial V - PvE Boss god 1
// (Extended Barrel, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling, Corkscrew Rifling, Fluted Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery, Ionized Battery), (Envious Assassin, Loose Change, Threat Detector), (Controlled Burst)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-God PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Nox Perennial V is a high impact fusion rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "strongest standout for body and ranged QoL DPS and a big step up over other fusions". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, and a ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when ADS and moving slower that 2.8m/s - crouch walking speed - as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are good by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/2366022261/nox-perennial-v and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2366022261 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The Recoil Direction is vertical but loose, but with only 5 bolts to control, you could look to boost Range with the barrel perk, which helps to a degree with recoil anyway, or to boost your preferred stat. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced or Ionized Battery gets you to 7 or the maximum of 8 shots respectively. High-impacts provide the best boss total damage and DPS among fusions. You can overflow the battery with Envious Assassin in the first trait perk column, which overflows the battery to up to ×3 capacity at 10% of mag size per kill with primary weapons and 20% with heavy (12.5%/22.5% when enhanced) before readying, which can preload almost all of your reserves. Otherwise there are reload parks like Loose Change to use with a heavy weapon or ability that applies an elemental debuff, which provides +50 Reload Speed +20 Aim Assist and ×1.2 ADS movement speed, for 9s (10s enhanced). or Threat Detector which procs per enemy within 15m up to ×2 stacks, with two stacks providing +40 Stability, +60 Reload Speed with a ×0.81 animation scalar and +100 Handling (+44, +64 and +100 respectively, enhanced). In the second column, for damage, Controlled Burst reduces charge time to ×0.9 and boosts damage by ×1.2 for 2s (2.5s when enhanced) after a kill or landing all the bolts in a burst. The Wild Card origin trait isn't that useful in a boss fight, providing the chance of Telesto-like "Experimental Submunitions" dropping on a kill - the number depends on the enemy tier - that last up to 10.5s and explode on contact or proximity to an enemy. If you're interested, "Nox Perennial" is a compound phrase derived from Latin, meaning "Everlasting Night" or "Enduring Darkness". Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-God PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Lionfish-4fr - PvE Utility god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Corkscrew Rifling, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Reconstruction), (Chill Clip)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-Champion PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE utility roll to deal with Overload and Unstoppable Champions in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly for Stasis builds. Lionfish-4fr is a rapid-fire fusion rifle, which are rated F-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being a "strict downgrade from high-impact and precision frames for boss damage". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the battery is empty as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are good by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/2423071981/lionfish-4fr and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2423071981 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. With 9 bolts to control, look for a barrel perk that helps with recoil control together with any other stat that you want to bump. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery gets you to the maximum of 7 shots. In the first trait perk column, there are some decent options but it's difficult to look past Reconstruction to refill 25% of the battery every 6s (5.5s when enhanced), overflowing to 200% of capacity. That pairs well in the second column with Chill Clip which applies +40 stacks of slow (freezing targets after 3 hits) within a 4m AoE for 4s on a hit - 8s with Whisper of Durance - with ammo in the top half of the base battery size. With Reconstruction, that means up to 12 Chill Clip shots. You'll already disrupt Overloads with the frame intrinsic, and shattering after freezing an Unstoppable will stun it, so you can handle two champion types with one weapon. The Problem Solver Origin Trait is very good for PvE when facing orange and yellow bars - including Champions - 11 bolts hitting (easy to do with two shots) will exhaust them and reduce damage output to ×0.75 for 5s and grant +50 to Reload Speed and Handling for 10s. Otherwise, Veist Stinger has a 10% chance to refill 25% of your base battery on a hit. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-Champion PvE-God M+KB Controller

Line in the Sand is a precision linear fusion rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic for "total damage standout, notable for solo due to less reliance on reload/Div vs. A499 and Particle access". The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/2450049485/line-in-the-sand and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2450049485 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. For a very niche roll, where there are constructs and vehicles present, such as the Threshers and Tank in Arms Dealer GMNF or Zoetic Lockset Shriekers in Sundered Doctrine, in the first trait perk column Deconstruct grants ×1.4 damage against vehicles and constructs and refills 10% of the battery from reserves after 3 hits within 6s, or 5 hits when the target isn't a vehicle or construct. In the second column, to boost damage further, Bait and Switch boosts damage by ×1.3 for 10s (11s when enhanced) for doing damage with your other two weapons, or Firing Line boosts crit damage by ×1.2 when you're within 15m of two or more allies in a fireteam. The Cast No Shadows origin trait refills 20% of the magazine and boosts handling on a melee hit, so isn't much use for this roll unfortunately. If you're interested, the name plays on the classic idiom of "drawing a line in the sand" - setting an absolute boundary that an enemy is forbidden to cross, reflected in the weapon's lore and flavour text. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Zealot's Reward - PvE Minor god 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Fluted Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Smallbore, Corkscrew Rifling, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Subsistence), (Reservoir Burst)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Zealot's Reward is a rapid-fire fusion rifle, which are rated F-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being a "strict downgrade from high-impact and precision frames for boss damage". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the battery is empty as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/2720651699/zealots-reward and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2720651699 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. With 9 bolts to control, look for a barrel perk that helps with recoil control together with any other stat that you want to bump. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery gets you to the maximum of 8 shots. For dealing with tougher Minor adds or the occasional orange or yellow bar, in the first trait perk column Subsistence reloads 10% of the battery (20% when enhanced) on kills, to keep the battery topped up to keep Reservoir Burst procced to provide a ×1.3 (×1.33 enhanced) damage boost when the battery is at maximum base capacity and triggers an AoE explosion on a kill, as well as passively granting +30 to Battery Capacity - the equivalent of a Backup Mag mod. The Photoinhibition origin trait is excellent in PvE, boosting shield, overshield and barrier damage, disorienting for 5s on a shield break, and AoE disorienting enemies in a 5m radius on a matching shield break. If you're interested, he name Zealot's Reward connects to the tragic lore of the fireteam of Rekkana, Yardarm-4, and Lisbon-13, who were seduced by the Black Garden. "Zealot" reflects their fanatical, corrupting obsession with the Garden's power, and the "reward" is the darkness artifacts and power they obtained, before turning on each other. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time or Backup Mag mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// (Arrowhead Brake, Fluted Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Smallbore, Corkscrew Rifling, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Auto-Loading Holster), (Withering Gaze)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice utility PvE roll for maintaining a Weaken debuff against bosses as part of a weapon rotation in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Void builds. Zealot's Reward is a rapid-fire fusion rifle, which are rated F-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being a "strict downgrade from high-impact and precision frames for boss damage". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the battery is empty as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium.
