For maintaining Weaken during boss damage phases, in the first trait perk column Auto-Loading Holster refills the battery 3.5s (3.3s when enhanced) after stowing. In the second column, Withering Gaze applies the Void Weaken debuff to targets after ADS-ing for 1.4s (1.3s enhanced) for ×1.15 damage for a refreshable 6s for your fireteam, but if the stronger ×1.3 weaken has been applied from Tractor Cannon or Shadowshot, a proc will reapply at the higher value. The Photoinhibition origin trait is excellent in PvE but less useful against bosses, boosting shield, overshield and barrier damage, disorienting for 5s on a shield break, and AoE disorienting enemies in a 5m radius on a matching shield break. If you're interested, he name Zealot's Reward connects to the tragic lore of the fireteam of Rekkana, Yardarm-4, and Lisbon-13, who were seduced by the Black Garden. "Zealot" reflects their fanatical, corrupting obsession with the Garden's power, and the "reward" is the darkness artifacts and power they obtained, before turning on each other. // Stormchaser - PvE Boss good 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Clown Cartridge, Auto-Loading Holster, Rapid Hit), (Precision Instrument, Firing Line)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Stormchaser is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/2862666249/stormchaser and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2862666249 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is right and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with a Backup Mag mod gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you insist, in the first trait perk column, Clown Cartridge overflows the battery by between ×1.1-1.5 rounded up (×1.13-1.5 when enhanced) randomly on a manual reload, Auto-Loading Holster refills the battery 3.5s (3.3s enhanced) after stowing so you always arrive at a damage phase with a full battery, and Rapid Hit stacks up to ×5 on crit hits for +25 Stability and +60 Reload Speed with a ×0.92 animation multiplier for a refreshable 2s. For damage, in the second column there's Precision Instrument grants escalating damage repeated crit hits up to a max ×1.25 at 6 stacks (×1.3 enhanced) for 1.25s or until you miss a crit or Firing Line boosts crit damage by ×1.2 when you're within 15m of two or more allies in a fireteam. The Bitterspite origin trait stacks up to ×5 on receiving damage which are consumed on reload to boost Reload Speed up to a max +50 (+55 enhanced) with a ×0.9 reload animation scalar. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Stormchaser - PvE Boss good 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Clown Cartridge, Auto-Loading Holster, Rapid Hit), (Elemental Honing)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Prismatic builds. Stormchaser is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/2862666249/stormchaser and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2862666249 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is right and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with a Backup Mag mod gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you insist, in the first trait perk column, Clown Cartridge overflows the battery by between ×1.1-1.5 rounded up (×1.13-1.5 when enhanced) randomly on a manual reload, Auto-Loading Holster refills the battery 3.5s (3.3s enhanced) after stowing so you always arrive at a damage phase with a full battery, and Rapid Hit stacks up to ×5 on crit hits for +25 Stability and +60 Reload Speed with a ×0.92 animation multiplier for a refreshable 2s. For damage and Prismatic synergy, in the second column Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to ×1.35 for 20s (23s enhanced) for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5, or Aggregate Charge provides a stack for each different elemental debuff up to ×3 stacks to boost damage by a max ×1.3 (×1.33 enhanced), with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active. The Bitterspite origin trait stacks up to ×5 on receiving damage which are consumed on reload to boost Reload Speed up to a max +50 (+55 enhanced) with a ×0.9 reload animation scalar. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Stormchaser - PvE Boss good 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Supercharged Magazine, Jolting Feedback), (Gear Shift, Aggregate Charge, Precision Instrument, Firing Line)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Arc builds. Stormchaser is an adaptive burst linear fusion rifle, which are rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being "worse DPS than even upper-middle special weapons and no access to ammo return perks". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, fires a 3-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/2862666249/stormchaser and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2862666249 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil direction is right and reasonably tight so you could improve recoil control or just look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery with a Backup Mag mod gets you the maximum of 7 shots. To be honest, adaptive burst LFRs are a weak subfamily for boss damage but, if you insist and are running an Arc build, in the first trait perk column Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the magazine every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff is active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity, or for a double damage roll, Jolting Feedback applies Arc Jolt after 4 hits (3 when enhanced or when amplified), triggering chain lightning on further hits for both target and AoE damage. For damage, in the second column there's Gear Shift which stacks up to ×5 on reloads after kills or becoming Amplified for a refreshable 15s, granting up to a max ×1.35 damage boost. Less Arc based for damage, there's Aggregate Charge to provide a stack for each different elemental debuff up to ×3 stacks to boost damage by a max ×1.3 (×1.33 enhanced), with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active, Precision Instrument to grant escalating damage repeated crit hits up to a max ×1.25 at 6 stacks (×1.3 enhanced) for 1.25s or until you miss a crit or Firing Line to boost crit damage by ×1.2 when you're within 15m of two or more allies in a fireteam. The Bitterspite origin trait stacks up to ×5 on receiving damage which are consumed on reload to boost Reload Speed up to a max +50 (+55 enhanced) with a ×0.9 reload animation scalar. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod, or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Techeun Force - PvE Boss good 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Corkscrew Rifling, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery, Ionized Battery), (Rewind Rounds, Reconstruction, Envious Assassin), (Aggregate Charge Controlled Burst)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS in a damage phase against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Techeun Force is as adaptive, which are rated F-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being a "strict downgrade from high-impact and precision frames for boss damage". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are good by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/3591141932/techeun-force and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3591141932 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The Recoil Direction is to the right and loose so look for a barrel perk that helps with recoil control together with any other stat that you want to bump. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced or Ionized Battery gets you 7 or the max of 8 shots respectively. While losing out to precision and high-impact frames for total boss damage, if you wanted one for ease of use, then in the first column, Rewind Rounds refills 60% of hits landed (70% when enhanced) from reserves when the battery empties. Otherwise, you could overflow the battery with Reconstruction to refill 25% of the battery every 6s (5.5s when enhanced), overflowing to 200% of capacity, or Envious Assassin to overflow the battery to up to ×3 capacity at 10% of mag size per kill with primary weapons and 20% with heavies (12.5%/22.5% when enhanced) before readying, to preload almost all of your reserves. In the second column,for damage, Aggregate Charge, particularly on a Prismatic build, provides a stack for each different elemental debuff up to ×3 stacks to boost damage by a max ×1.3 (×1.33 enhanced), with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active. Otherwise, Controlled Burst reduces charge time to ×0.9 and boosts damage by ×1.2 for 2s (2.5s when enhanced) after a kill or landing all the bolts in a burst. The Explosive Pact origin trait stacks up to ×5 on grenade ability usage to boost Stability and Reload Speed for 7.5s, so isn't that useful for boss damage, unfortunately. If you're interested, the name directly connects to Awoken tech-witches and Queen Mara Sov's inner circle, representing the channeled paracausal energy of the Techeuns. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Charge Time mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): Backup PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Cartesian Coordinate is a rapid-fire fusion rifle, which are rated F-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic being a "strict downgrade from high-impact and precision frames for boss damage". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the battery is empty as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are good by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/3719824177/cartesian-coordinate and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3719824177 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. With 9 bolts to control, look for a barrel perk that helps with recoil control together with any other stat that you want to bump. Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage or Enhanced Battery in the battery perk slot gets you to the maximum of 8 shots. While losing out to precision and high-impact frames for total boss damage, if you wanted one for ease of use, in the first trait perk slot, either Envious Arsenal refills the battery from reserves on dealing damage with both of your other weapons as part of a damage weapon rotation, or Reconstruction refills 25% of the battery every 6s (5.5s when enhanced) up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. In the second column for damage, Aggregate Charge, particularly on a Prismatic build, provides a stack for each different elemental debuff up to ×3 stacks to boost damage by a max ×1.3 (×1.33 when enhanced), with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active, or Controlled Burst reduces charge time to ×0.9 and boosts damage by ×1.2 for 2s (2.5s enhanced) after a kill or landing all the bolts in a burst. Neither origin trait is particularly useful for a boss roll - Veteran's Wisdom procs on a crit kill or 3 kills within 3s of each other to generate an 8m (9m enhanced) AoE kinetic shockwave which inflicts Exhaust on enemies. There's also Field-Tested which starts subtly but if you can stay alive to get hits and kills, you'll start to feel the boosts to Range, Stability, Handling and Reload Speed as you get to the higher stacks. If you're interested, in mathematics a Cartesian coordinate system specifies each point uniquely in a plane by a set of numerical coordinates. // Eyes Unveiled - PvE Boss god 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Fourth Time's the Charm, Overflow), (Elemental Honing)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvP-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly on Prismatic builds. Eyes Unveiled is a precision linear fusion rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic for "total damage standout, notable for solo due to less reliance on reload/Div vs. A499 and Particle access". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/4147428506/eyes-unveiled and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/4147428506 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. With precision frames, you need to worry less about recoil control so look to improve Handling or maybe Stability. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery gets you to the max 7 shots. For sustained boss damage, in the first trait perk column, Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 2s (3s when enhanced) of each other - that's 10 shots from a battery of 6, or 11 from a battery of 7, with 4 of them free. Less ammo efficient, but also good is Overflow which refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2 of base (×2.2 when enhanced), rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick. In the second column, for damage on a Prismatic build, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to an max ×1.35 for 20s (23s when enhanced) for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. The Heretical Behaviour origin trait is excellent for boss damage, proccing for a refreshable 8s after 4 hits within 3s or for for 21s after being revived (10s and 23s enhanced) to boost damage by ×1.08, and Handling and Reload Speed by +50 with a ×0.8 reload animation scalar. Alternatively The Willing Vessel origin trait generates up to 5 stacks of a slowly decaying buff on dealing damage and kills, to boost Stability, Handling and Reload Speed. If you're interested, the name connects to the theatrical framing and truth-revealing motifs of Episode: Heresy. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvP-God M+KB Controller

// Eyes Unveiled - PvE Boss god 2
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Accelerated Coils, Enhanced Battery), (Fourth Time's the Charm, Overflow), (Precision Instrument)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvP-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for sustained DPS against a single yellow-bar target in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Eyes Unveiled is a precision linear fusion rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic tiering spreadsheet https://linktr.ee/TheAegisRelic for "total damage standout, notable for solo due to less reliance on reload/Div vs. A499 and Particle access". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/4147428506/eyes-unveiled and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/4147428506 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. With precision frames, you need to worry less about recoil control so look to improve Handling or maybe Stability. As the battery perk, Accelerated Coils improves charge time by 40ms at the cost of ×0.98 base damage, or Enhanced Battery gets you to the max 7 shots. For sustained boss damage, in the first trait perk column, Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 2s (3s when enhanced) of each other - that's 10 shots from a battery of 6, or 11 from a battery of 7, with 4 of them free. Less ammo efficient, but also good is Overflow which refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2 of base (×2.2 when enhanced), rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick. In the second column, for damage, Precision Instrument grants up to ×6 stacks of escalating damage on repeated crit hits up to a max ×1.25 (×1.3 enhanced) for 1.25s or until you miss a crit. The Heretical Behaviour origin trait is excellent for boss damage, proccing for a refreshable 8s after 4 hits within 3s or for for 21s after being revived (10s and 23s enhanced) to boost damage by ×1.08, and Handling and Reload Speed by +50 with a ×0.8 reload animation scalar. Alternatively The Willing Vessel origin trait generates up to 5 stacks of a slowly decaying buff on dealing damage and kills, to boost Stability, Handling and Reload Speed. If you're interested, the name connects to the theatrical framing and truth-revealing motifs of Episode: Heresy. Recommended MW: Charge Time, with a Backup Mag or Charge Time mod or your preferred choice to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-SustainedDamage PvE-EndGame PvP-God M+KB Controller
